2024 年，國內外經濟形勢複雜，在多重因素的影   In 2024, amid a complex domestic and international economic
響下，氟硅化工行業變化較大。在該情況下，行      condition, the fluorosilicon chemical industry underwent significant
業內部分產品依然延續著上一年較弱的市場狀態，     changes as affected by multiple factors. Given such circumstances,
但也有部分產品因外部環境原因價格大幅上漲。      market demand for some products within the industry remained weak,
本集團憑藉在氟硅化工行業多年來經營積累的優      similar to the previous year, while others experienced substantial price
勢，迅速把握市場機遇，為集團的業績帶來了一      increases due to external market factors. Leveraging on the strengths
定的增長。                      accumulated over years of operation in the fluorosilicon chemical
                           industry, the Group rapidly seized market opportunities and recorded
                           a certain level of performance growth.

匯率波動風險及相關對沖活動                              Exposure to Fluctuations in Exchange Rates and Related Hedges

本集團的功能貨幣為人民幣，且大多數交易以人                      The Group’s functional currency is RMB, and most transactions
民幣結算。然而，本集團自海外客戶賺取收益及                      are settled in RMB. However, foreign currencies (mainly United
向海外供應商購買機器及設備結算時均收取╱支                      States dollars) are received/paid when the Group earns revenue
付外幣（主要為美元）。                                from overseas customers, and settles purchases of machinery and
                                           equipment to overseas suppliers.

為減少持有外幣的風險，本集團經考慮不久將來                      To reduce the holding risk of foreign currencies, the Group normally
的外幣付款安排後，通常會在款項收訖後隨即將                      converts the foreign currencies into RMB upon payment receipt after
外幣兌換成人民幣。                                  taking into account its foreign currencies payment schedule in the
                                           near future.

東岳集團有限公司（「本公司」）董事會（「董事會」）                 The board of Directors (the “Board”) of Dongyue Group Limited
肩負為本公司股東（「股東」）創造價值及帶來最大                   (the “Company”) recognizes their mission of creating values and
回報的使命，同時履行其企業責任。為此，本公                     maximizing returns to the shareholders of the Company (the
司矢志提升及保持最高水準的企業管治。                        “Shareholders”), while at the same time fulfilling their corporate
                                          responsibilities. To this end, we strive to promote and uphold the
                                          highest standard of corporate governance.

董事會已檢討本公司的企業管治常規，並採納及                     The Board has reviewed the corporate governance practices of
改進各項程序及文書處理方式，詳情載於本報告。                    the Company with the adoption and improvement of the various
據 董 事 所 盡 知 及 盡 信，本 公 司 於 截 至 2024 年 12   procedures and documentation which are detailed in this report. To
月 31 日止年度及截至本報告日期採納並一直遵                   the best knowledge and belief of the Directors, the Company has
守 上 市 規 則 附 錄 十 四 所 載 的 企 業 管 治 守 則（「守    adopted and complied with the code provisions of the Corporate
則」）的守則條文，惟關於主席與行政總裁的角色                    Governance Code contained in Appendix 14 of the Listing Rules
偏離守則除外，有關詳情披露於本報告下文相關                     (the “Code”) during the year ended 31 December 2024 and up to
段落。                                       the date of this report, except for deviation regarding the roles of
                                          chairman and chief executive officer, the details of which have been
                                          disclosed in the relevant paragraphs below in this report.

董事會                                       The Board

稱「本集團」）及監察本集團的業務、策略性方針                    and its subsidiaries (together the “Group”), and oversees the Group’s
和表現。董事會轉授權力和責任予本集團管理人                     businesses, strategic direction and performance. The Board delegates
員，以管理本集團。此外，董事會亦已轉授若干                     the authority and responsibility to the Group’s management for the

的要求。                                      of expertise, skills and experience which can meet the requirements of

董事的委任及重選                                  Appointment and re-election of Directors
新董事的委任及其他相關事項已委派予本公司提                     The appointment of new Director(s) and other related matters have
名委員會負責。提名委員會就相關事項（包括本                     been delegated to the Company’s Nomination Committee, which
公司董事的委任及重新委任）向董事會提供建議。                    would make recommendations to the Board on relevant matters
董事會認為，預期董事應具備有關範疇的專業知                     including the appointment or reappointment of the Directors of the
識，以為本公司作出貢獻，且有足夠時間參與本                     Company. The Board considers that the Director(s) is expected to have
公司的決策程序。                                  expertise in relevant area to make contribution to the Company and
                                          to have sufficient time to participate in the decision making process of
                                          the Company.

據本公司章程細則輪值並重選連任。                          the Group but subject to rotation and re-election in accordance with
                                          the Company’s articles association.

分之一董事，或倘董事人數並非三之倍數，則最                     one-third of the Directors for the time being (or if the number is not a
接近但不少於三分之一人數，須於各股東週年大                     multiple of three, then the number nearest to but not less than one-
會按照章程細則條文輪值退任。因此，全體董事                     third) shall retire from office by rotation at each AGM in accordance
均須依據章程細則於股東週年大會輪值退任並重                     with the provisions of the Articles. Accordingly, all Directors shall be
選連任。                                      subject to retirement by rotation and re-election at AGM under the

主席與行政總裁的角色                                Roles of chairman and chief executive officer
守 則 第 二 部 分 第 C.2.1 條 規 定，主 席 與 行 政 總 裁   Code C.2.1 of Part 2 of the Code stipulates that the roles of chairman
的角色應有區分，且不應由同一人兼任。張建宏                     and chief executive officer should be separate and should not be
先生目前身兼本公司主席與行政總裁。董事會認                     performed by the same individual. Mr. Zhang Jianhong is both the
為，由一人同時兼任主席及行政總裁可令本集團                     Chairman and Chief Executive Officer of the Company. The Board
更能貫徹強勢領導，且在業務策劃上更具效率。                     believes that vesting the roles of both Chairman and Chief Executive
此外，董事會認為，在由多名經驗豐富且具才幹                     Officer in the same position provides the Group with stronger and
的人士所組成董事會密切監控下，此架構不會影                     more consistent leadership and allows for more effective planning.
響權力平衡。董事會對張先生充滿信心，並深信                     Further, the Board considers that this structure will not impair the
現時安排對本集團的業務前景最為有利。                        balance of power, which has been closely monitored by the Board,
                                          which comprises experienced and high caliber individuals. The
                                          Board has full confidence in Mr. Zhang and believes that the current
                                          arrangement is beneficial to the business prospect of the Group.

提名委員會（續）                                 Nomination committee (Continued)
此外，有關董事會成員多元化政策的概要載列如                    In addition, a summary of the Policy for the diversity of the Board is
下：                                       set out as follows:

A.   政策聲明                                A.   Policy Statement
     本公司明白並深信建立一個多元的董事會                       The Company recognizes and embraces the benefits of having a
     的 裨 益 良 多。一 個 真 正 多 元 的 董 事 會 可 容        diverse Board. A truly diverse Board will include and make good
     入 並 善 用 董 事 的 不 同 技 能、地 區 及 行 業 經        use of differences in the skills, regional and industry experience,
     驗、背景、種族、性別及其他資歷。此等差                      background, race, gender and other qualities of Directors.
     異 將 用 作 決 定 董 事 會 的 最 佳 組 合。董 事 會        These differences will be taken into account in determining the
     所 有 的 委 任 均 以 用 人 唯 才 為 原 則，並 於 考        optimum composition of the Board. All Board appointments
     慮人選時以客觀條件充分顧及董事會成員                       will be based on meritocracy, and candidates will be considered
     多元化的裨益。                                  against objective criteria, having due regard for the benefits of
                                              diversity of the Board.

意見                                      Opinion
吾等已審計第 138 至 258 頁所載東岳集團有限公             We have audited the consolidated financial statements of Dongyue
司（「貴 公 司」）及 其附 屬 公司（統 稱「貴集 團」）的         Group Limited (the “Company”) and its subsidiaries (collectively
綜合財務報表，當中包括於 2024 年 12 月 31 日的          referred to as the “Group”) set out on pages 138 to 258, which
綜合財務狀況表及截至該日止年度的綜合損益及                   comprise the consolidated statement of financial position as at 31
其他全面收入報表、綜合權益變動表及綜合現金                   December 2024, and the consolidated statement of profit or loss and
流量表，以及綜合財務報表附註，包括重大會計                   other comprehensive income, consolidated statement of changes in
政策資料。                                   equity and consolidated statement of cash flows for the year then
                                        ended, and notes to the consolidated financial statements, including
                                        material accounting policy information.

吾等認為，綜合財務報表已遵照國際會計準則理                   In our opinion, the consolidated financial statements give a true and
事 會（「國 際 會 計 準 則 理 事 會」）頒 佈 的 國 際 財 務   fair view of the consolidated financial position of the Group as at 31
報告準則會計準則真實公平地反映 貴集團於                    December 2024, and of its consolidated financial performance and its
2024 年 12 月 31 日之綜合財務狀況，及其於截至           consolidated cash flows for the year then ended in accordance with
該日止年度之綜合財務表現及綜合現金流量及該                   IFRS Accounting Standards issued by the International Accounting

關鍵審計事項                                      Key Audit Matters
關鍵審計事項是根據吾等的專業判斷，認為對當                       Key audit matter is the matter that, in our professional judgment,
前期間的綜合財務報表的審計最為重要的事項。                       was of most significance in our audit of the consolidated financial
該事項是在吾等審計整體綜合財務報表及出具意                       statements of the current period. This matter was addressed in the
見時進行處理的，吾等不會對該事項提供單獨的                       context of our audit of the consolidated financial statements as a
意見。                                         whole, and in forming our opinion thereon, and we do not provide a
                                            separate opinion on this matter.

1.   貿易應收款項                                 1.   Trade receivables

     茲提述綜合財務報表附註 25。                             Refer to Note 25 to the consolidated financial statements.

     於 2024 年 12 月 31 日， 貴 集 團 持 有 貿 易 應         As at 31 December 2024, the Group had gross trade receivables
     收 款 項 總 額 約 人 民 幣 1,962,821,000 元（包         of approximately RMB1,962,821,000 (including bills receivables
     括 應 收 票 據 人 民 幣 1,579,632,000 元）及 減         amounting to RMB1,579,632,000) and provision for impairment
     值撥備約人民幣 12,876,000 元。                       of approximately RMB12,876,000.

     一般而言， 貴集團授予客戶應收賬款的信                         In general, the credit terms granted by the Group to the
     貸期介乎 30 至 90 天。管理層基於以下資料                    customers ranged between 30 to 90 days for trade receivables.
     對貿易應收款項的可收回程度及減值撥備                          Management performed periodic assessment on the
     的 充 足 性 進 行 定 期 評 估，包 括 不 同 客 戶 的           recoverability of the trade receivables and the sufficiency of
     信 用 狀 況、貿 易 應 收 款 項 賬 齡、歷 史 結 算             provision for impairment based on information including credit
     記 錄、後 續 結 算 情 況、變 現 未 收 回 結 餘 的             profile of different customers, ageing of the trade receivables,
     預期時間和金額及與有關客戶的長期貿易                          historical settlement records, subsequent settlement status,
     關 係。管 理 層 亦 考 慮 可 能 影 響 客 戶 償 還 未           expected timing and amount of realisation of outstanding
     收 回 結 餘 能 力 的 前 瞻 性 資 料，從 而 就 減 值           balances, and ongoing trading relationships with the relevant
     評估估計預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）。                       customers. Management also considered forward-looking
                                                 information that may impact the customers’ ability to repay the

     吾等將貿易應收款項識別為關鍵審計事項，                         We identified trade receivables as a key audit matter due to

     斷 及 估 計，包 括 信 貸 風 險 是 否 大 幅 增 加、            judgements, including determination of whether there is
     釐 定 違 約 概 率 時 是 否 應 用 模 式 及 假 設，以           significant increase in credit risk, use of models and assumptions

(a)   股份溢價                                             (a)      Share premium

      根據開曼群島公司法（經修訂），本公司的股份溢價賬                                  Under the Companies Law (Revised) of the Cayman Islands, the share premium
      可分配給本公司所有者，惟在緊接建議分派股息之日後，                                 of the Company is distributable to the owners of the Company provided
      本公司將有能力償還其在日常業務過程中到期的債務。                                  that immediately following the date on which the dividend is proposed to be
                                                                distributed, the Company will be in a position to pay off its debts as they fall due
                                                                in the ordinary course of business.

(b)   按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備                              (b)      Equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve

      按公平值計入其他全面收益的股本工具儲備包括於報告                                  The equity instruments at FVTOCI reserve comprises the cumulative net change
      期末根據國際財務報告準則第 9 號持有之按公平值計入                                in the fair value of equity instruments at FVTOCI under IFRS 9 that are held at the
      其他全面收益之股本工具之公平值累計淨變動。                                     end of the reporting period.

(c)   合併儲備                                             (c)      Merger reserve

      合併儲備來自於 2006 年完成之集團重組。                                    Merger reserve arose in group reorganisation completed in 2006.

(d)   股本儲備                                             (d)      Capital reserve

      於 2007 年 11 月 16 日，本公司購回所有早前已發行每股                         On 16 November 2007, the Company repurchased all of the 275,000,000
      面值 0.1 美元之普通股 275,000,000 股，而該等購回普通                       previously issued ordinary shares of US$0.1 each and these repurchased ordinary
      股與截至該日期之所有法定但未發行股本一併註銷。同                                  shares were cancelled with all of the authorised but unissued share capital as
      日，藉增設 4,000,000,000 股每股面值 0.1 港元之新普通                      of that date. On the same date, the authorised share capital was increased to
      股，將法定股本增至 400,000,000 港元。於 2007 年 11 月                    HK$400,000,000 by the creation of 4,000,000,000 new ordinary shares of
      15 日，向 現 有 股 東 發 行 275,000,000 股 每 股 面 值 0.1 港            HK$0.1 each. 275,000,000 new ordinary shares of HK$0.1 each were then issued
      元之新普通股。購回金額超過已發行新股份面值之款額                                  to the shareholders existing on 15 November 2007. The excess of the repurchased
      直接計入股本儲備。向附屬公司非控股股東收購額外權                                  amount over the nominal amount of new shares issued was credited directly to
      益確認為與非控股股東權益之交易，因此產生之相關折                                  the capital reserve. The acquisitions of additional interest from non-controlling
      讓╱溢價直接於股本儲備列賬╱支銷。                                         shareholders of subsidiaries were recognised as transactions with non-controlling
                                                                interests and the corresponding discount/premium were credited/debited directly
                                                                against capital reserve.

(e)   安全儲備                                             (e)      Safety reserve

      根據中華人民共和國（「中國」）財政部及國家安全生產                                 Pursuant to certain regulations issued by the Ministry of Finance and the State
      監督管理總局頒佈的若干法規，本集團之若干中國附屬                                  Administration of Work Safety of the People’s Republic of China (“PRC”), some of
      公司須以累進比率按危險化學品銷售總收入之 0.5% 至                               the Group’s PRC subsidiaries are required to set aside an amount of safety reserve
      4% 預留一定數額之安全儲備金。該儲備可用作本集團                                 at progressive rates from 0.5% to 4% of the total revenue from the sales of
      日常運作於安全生產改善及維修之用，被視為開支性質，                                 hazardous chemical. The reserve can be utilised for the spending in improvements
      並於產生時計入損益。                                                and maintenances of work safety on the Group’s daily operations, which are
                                                                considered expenses in nature and charged to the profit and loss as incurred.

(f)   法定盈餘儲備                                           (f)      Statutory surplus reserve

      根據中國公司法及相關公司組織章程細則，本公司中國                                  In accordance with the Company Law of PRC and the relevant Articles of
      附屬公司須將相等於根據中國會計準則釐定之除稅後溢                                  Association, the PRC subsidiaries of the Company are required to appropriate
      利 10% 之金額撥入法定盈餘儲備。                                        amount equal to 10% of their profit after taxation as determined in accordance
                                                                with the PRC accounting standards to the statutory surplus reserve.

      法定盈餘儲備為股東權益之一部分，當其結餘達到相當                                  Statutory surplus reserve is part of shareholders’ equity and when its balance
      於 註 冊 資 本 50% 之 金 額，則 毋 須 進 一 步 分 配。根 據 中                 reaches an amount equal to 50% of the registered capital, further appropriation
      國公司法，法定盈餘儲備可用作填補過往虧損、增加生                                  is not required. According to the Company Law of the PRC, statutory surplus
      產及業務營運或轉增股本。                                              reserve may be used to make up past losses, to increase production and business
                                                                operations or to increase capital by means of conversion.

      Grand Pavilion, Hibiscus Way, 802 West Bay        31119, Grand Pavilion, Hibiscus Way, 802 West Bay Road,

      附屬公司（合稱「本集團」）主要從事製造、                              and its subsidiaries (collectively referred to as the “Group”) are
      分 銷 及 銷 售 高 分 子 材 料、有 機 硅、製 冷 劑                   principally engaged in the manufacture, distribution and sale

3.    重大會計政策                              3.   Material accounting policies
      報告編製依據                                   Basis of preparation

      綜合財務報表乃根據國際會計準則理事會                       The consolidated financial statements have been prepared
      頒佈的國際財務報告準則會計準則編製。                       in accordance with IFRS Accounting Standards issued by
      另 外，綜 合 財 務 報 表 包 括 香 港 聯 合 交 易 所        International Accounting Standards Board. In addition, the
      有限公司證券上市規則及香港公司條例規                       consolidated financial statements include applicable disclosures
      定之適用披露。                                  required by the Rules Governing the Listing of Securities on The
                                               Stock Exchange of Hong Kong Limited and by the Hong Kong
                                               Companies Ordinance.

      綜 合 財 務 報 表 乃 按 歷 史 成 本 基 準 編 製，惟        The consolidated financial statements have been prepared
      金融工具於各報告期末乃按公平值計入損                       on the historical cost basis except for financial instruments
      益及按公平值計入其他全面收益計量除外。                      that are measured at fair value through profit or loss and fair
      歷史成本一般按交換貨品及服務之代價之                       value through other comprehensive income at the end of each
      公平值計算。                                   reporting period. Historical cost is generally based on the fair
                                               value of the consideration given in exchange for goods and
                                               services

      公平值指於計量日期市場參與者於有秩序                       Fair value is the price that would be received to sell an asset
      交易中出售一項資產可收取或轉讓一項負                       or paid to transfer a liability in an orderly transaction between
      債 須 支 付 之 價 格，不 論 該 價 格 是 否 直 接 可        market participants at the measurement date, regardless of
      觀 察 或 可 使 用 其 他 估 值 方 法 作 出 估 計。倘        whether that price is directly observable or estimated using
      市場參與者於計量日期對資產或負債定價                       another valuation technique. In estimating the fair value
      時 會 考 慮 資 產 或 負 債 的 特 點，則 本 集 團 於        of an asset or a liability, the Group takes into account the
      估計資產或負債的公平值時會考慮該等特                       characteristics of the asset or liability if market participants
      點。該 等 綜 合 財 務 報 表 中 作 計 量 及╱或 披          would take those characteristics into account when pricing
      露 用 途 的 公 平 值 乃 按 此 基 準 釐 定，惟 屬 於        the asset or liability at the measurement date. Fair value for
      國際財務報告準則第2號以股份支付款項                       measurement and/or disclosure purposes in these consolidated
      範 圍 的 以 股 份 付 款 的 交 易、屬 於 國 際 財 務        financial statements is determined on such a basis, except for
      報告準則第 16 號範圍內的租賃交易，以及                    share-based payment transactions that are within the scope
      與公平值有部分相若地方但並非公平值的                       of IFRS 2 Share-based Payment, leasing transactions that are
      計量，譬如國際會計準則第 2 號存貨內的可                    accounted for in accordance with IFRS 16, and measurements
      變現淨值或國際會計準則第 36 號資產減值                    that have some similarities to fair value but are not fair value,
      的使用價值除外。                                 such as net realisable value in IAS 2 Inventories or value in use in

      此 外，出 於 財 務 報 告 目 的，公 平 值 計 量 應          In addition, for financial reporting purposes, fair value
      基於公平值計量的輸入值的可觀察程度以                       measurements are categorised into Level 1, 2 or 3 based on the
      及該等輸入值對整體公平值計量的重要性，                      degree to which the inputs to the fair value measurements are
      被 歸 入 第 一 層、第 二 層 或 第 三 層 的 公 平 值        observable and the significance of the inputs to the fair value
      級次，詳述如下：                                 measurement in its entirety, which are described as follows:

      第一層輸入值是指於計量日期由相同資產                       Level 1 inputs are quoted prices (unadjusted) in active markets
      或負債在活躍市場中（未經調整）的報價得                      for identical assets or liabilities that the entity can access at the
      出；第 二 層 輸 入 值 是 指 由 除 了 第 一 層 輸 入        measurement date; Level 2 inputs are inputs, other than quoted
      值 所 包 含 的 報 價 以 外 的，資 產 或 負 債 可 由        prices included within Level 1, that are observable for the asset
      直 接 或 間 接 觀 察 得 出 的 輸 入 值；及 第 三 層        or liability, either directly or indirectly; and Level 3 inputs are
      輸入值是指資產或負債的不可觀察輸入值。                      unobservable inputs for the asset or liability.

      重大會計政策載列如下。                              The material accounting policies are set out below.

      綜合基準                                     Basis of consolidation

      綜合財務報表包括本公司及由本公司及其                       The consolidated financial statements incorporate the financial
      附 屬 公 司 控 制 的 實 體 的 財 務 報 表。當 本 公        statements of the Company and entities controlled by the
      司具備以下條件時，即具有控制權：                         Company and its subsidiaries. Control is achieved when the
                                               Company:

      —     有控制被投資方的權力；                        —     has power over the investee;

      —     可或有權自其與被投資方的關係中獲                   —     is exposed, or has rights, to variable returns from its
            得不同回報；及                                  involvement with the investee; and

      —     有能力運用其權力影響其回報。                     —     has the ability to use its power to affect its returns.

      倘事實和情況顯示上述的三項控制因素之                       The Group reassesses whether or not it controls an investee if
      一 項 或 多 項 出 現 變 動，本 集 團 會 重 新 評 估        facts and circumstances indicate that there are changes to one
      其是否控制被投資方。                               or more of the three elements of control listed above.

      附屬公司於本集團取得該附屬公司的控制                       Consolidation of a subsidiary begins when the Group obtains
      權 時 綜 合 入 賬，並 於 本 集 團 喪 失 對 該 附 屬        control over the subsidiary and ceases when the Group loses
      公司的控制權時終止綜合入賬。具體而言，                      control of the subsidiary. Specifically, income and expenses of a
      自本集團取得控制權當日起直至本集團不                       subsidiary acquired or disposed of during the year are included
      再 控 制 附 屬 公 司 當 日，於 年 內 收 購 或 出 售        in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
      的附屬公司收支均計入綜合損益及其他全                       comprehensive income from the date the Group gains control
      面收入報表。                                   until the date when the Group ceases to control the subsidiary.

      客戶合約收益                                   Revenue from contracts with customers

      本 集 團 於 完 成 履 約 責 任 時，即 於 特 定 履 約        The Group recognises revenue when (or as) a performance
      責任相關的貨品或服務的「控制權」轉讓予                      obligation is satisfied, i.e. when “control” of the goods or
      客戶時確認收益。                                 services underlying the particular performance obligation is
                                               transferred to the customer.

      履約責任指可明確區分的一項貨品或服務                       A performance obligation represents a good or service (or a
     （或 一組 貨品 或 服務），或 一 系列 大 致 相 同             bundle of goods or services) that is distinct or a series of distinct
      的可明確區分的貨品或服務。                            goods or services that are substantially the same.

      倘 符 合 以 下 其 中 一 項 標 準，則 控 制 權 隨 時        Control is transferred over time and revenue is recognised over
      間 轉 移，而 收 益 則 參 照 完 全 履 行 相 關 履 約        time by reference to the progress towards complete satisfaction
      責任的進展情況而隨時間確認：                           of the relevant performance obligation if one of the following
                                               criteria is met:

      —     客戶在本集團履約的同時即取得並消                   —     the customer simultaneously receives and consumes the
            耗本集團履約所帶來之利益；                            benefits provided by the Group’s performance as the
                                                     Group performs;

      —     本集團履約導致產生或提升一項資產，                  —     the Group’s performance creates or enhances an asset
            而該項資產於產生或提升時由客戶控                         that the customer controls as the Group performs; or
            制；或

      —     本集團履約未產生對本集團具有替代                   —     the Group’s performance does not create an asset with
            用途之資產，而本集團有執行權收取                         an alternative use to the Group and the Group has an
            迄今已履約部分之款項。                              enforceable right to payment for performance completed
                                                     to date.

      否 則，收 益 於 客 戶 獲 得 特 定 貨 品 或 服 務 的        Otherwise, revenue is recognised at a point in time when the
      控制權時確認。                                  customer obtains control of the distinct good or service.

      租賃                                       Leases

      倘合約授予權利以代價為交換在某一時期                       A contract is, or contains, a lease if the contract conveys the
      內 控 制 使 用 已 識 別 資 產，則 該 合 約 屬 於 租        right to control the use of an identified asset for a period of
      賃或包含租賃。                                  time in exchange for consideration.

      就於國際財務報告準則第 16 號初步應用日                    For contracts entered into or modified on or after the date
      期或之後訂立或修訂或由業務合併產生的                       of initial application of IFRS 16 or arising from business
      合 約 而 言，本 集 團 根 據 國 際 財 務 報 告 準 則        combinations, the Group assesses whether a contract is or
      第 16 號 中 的 定 義，於 開 始、修 訂 日 期 或 收         contains a lease based on the definition under IFRS 16 at
      購日期（倘合適）評估合約是否屬於租賃或                      inception, modification date or acquisition date, as appropriate.
      包 含 租 賃。除 非 合 約 條 款 及 條 件 其 後 出 現        Such contract will not be reassessed unless the terms and
      變動，否則有關合約將不予重新評估。                        conditions of the contract are subsequently changed.

      本集團作為承租人                                 The Group as a lessee
      就 可 行 實 際 權 宜 方 法 而 言，當 本 集 團 合 理        As a practical expedient, leases with similar characteristics are
      預期對綜合財務報表的影響將不會與投資                       accounted on a portfolio basis when the Group reasonably
      組 合 內 個 別 租 賃 有 重 大 差 異 時，具 有 相 似        expects that the effects on the consolidated financial statements
      特徵的租賃將以組合為基礎入賬。                          would not differ materially from individual leases within the
                                               portfolio.

      使用權資產                                    Right-of-use assets
      使用權資產的成本包括：                              The cost of right-of-use asset includes:

      —     租賃負債的初步計量金額；                       —     the amount of the initial measurement of the lease

      借貸成本                                     Borrowing costs

      借 貸 成 本 直 接 源 自 收 購、建 造 或 生 產 合 資        Borrowing costs directly attributable to the acquisition,
      格 資 產，而 該 等 資 產 需 要 一 段 長 時 間 方 可        construction or production of qualifying assets, which are assets
      供 用 作 擬 定 用 途 或 出 售。借 貸 成 本 會 加 入        that necessarily take a substantial period of time to get ready
      作 該 等 資 產 的 成 本，直 至 資 產 大 致 上 可 供        for their intended use or sale, are added to the cost of those

      算 一 般 借 款 的 資 本 化 率。尚 未 用 於 合 資 格        in the general borrowing pool for calculation of capitalisation

      入，於合資格資本化的借貸成本中扣除。                       temporary investment of specific borrowings pending their
                                               expenditure on qualifying assets is deducted from the borrowing

      稅項                                       Taxation

      所得稅開支指即期應付稅項及遞延稅項的                       Income tax expense represents the sum of the tax currently
      總和。                                      payable and deferred tax.

      即期應付稅項按本年度應課稅溢利計算。                       The tax currently payable is based on taxable profit for the
      應課稅溢利與綜合損益及其他全面收入報                       year. Taxable profit differs from profit before tax as reported
      表 中 呈 報 的 稅 前 溢 利 不 同，此 乃 由 於 其 他        in the consolidated statement of profit or loss and other
      年 度 的 應 課 稅 或 可 扣 稅 收 入 或 開 支，以 及        comprehensive income because income or expense that
      從 未 課 稅 或 可 扣 稅 的 項 目 所 致。本 集 團 的        are taxable or deductible in other years and items that are
      即期稅項負債採用於報告期末已制定或實                       never taxable or deductible. The Group’s liability for current
      質制定的稅率計算。                                tax is calculated using tax rates that have been enacted or
                                               substantively enacted by the end of the reporting period.

      遞延稅項按綜合財務報表的資產及負債賬                       Deferred tax is recognised on temporary differences between
      面值與用以計算應課稅溢利相應稅基的暫                       the carrying amounts of assets and liabilities in the consolidated
      時 差 額 確 認。遞 延 稅 項 負 債 通 常 會 就 所 有        financial statements and the corresponding tax base used in
      應 課 稅 暫 時 差 額 確 認。遞 延 稅 項 資 產 一 般        the computation of taxable profit. Deferred tax liabilities are
      在很可能出現可利用暫時差額扣稅的應課                       generally recognised for all taxable temporary differences.
      稅 溢 利 時 就 所 有 可 扣 減 暫 時 差 額 確 認。若        Deferred tax assets are generally recognised for all deductible
      於 一 項 交 易 中，因 商 譽 或 因 業 務 合 併 以 外        temporary difference to the extent that it is probable that
      原因首次確認其他資產及負債而引致的暫                       taxable profits will be available against which those deductible
      時 差 額 既 不 影 響 應 課 稅 溢 利，亦 不 影 響 會        temporary differences can be utilised. Such assets and liabilities
      計溢利，則不會確認該等資產及負債。                        are not recognised if the temporary difference arises from
                                               goodwill or from the initial recognition (other than in a business

                                               affects neither the taxable profit nor the accounting profit.

      遞延稅項負債乃按因於附屬公司及聯營公                       Deferred tax liabilities are recognised for taxable temporary
      司的投資而引致的應課稅暫時差額而確認，                      differences associated with investments in subsidiaries and
      惟若本集團可控制暫時差額的撥回及暫時                       associates, except where the Group is able to control the
      差額很有可能不會於可見將來撥回的情況                       reversal of the temporary difference and it is probable that
      除 外。因 與 有 關 投 資 及 權 益 相 關 的 可 扣 減        the temporary difference will not reverse in the foreseeable
      暫時差額而產生的遞延稅項資產僅在可能                       future. Deferred tax assets arising from deductible temporary
      產生足夠應課稅溢利以動用暫時差額利益                       differences associated with such investments and interests are
      並預期可在可見將來撥回時確認。                          only recognised to the extent that it is probable that there will

      於聯營公司的權益                                 Interest in an associate

      聯營公司為本集團對其有重大影響力的實                       An associate is an entity over which the Group has significant
      體。重 大 影 響 力 指 有 權 參 與 被 投 資 方 的 財        influence. Significant influence is the power to participate in the
      務 及 經 營 政 策 的 決 策，但 對 該 等 政 策 並 無        financial and operating policy decisions of the investee but is
      控制或共同控制權。                                not control or joint control over those policies.

      聯 營 公 司 之 業 績、資 產 及 負 債 使 用 權 益 會        The results and assets and liabilities of associates are
      計 法 計 入 此 等 綜 合 財 務 報 表。以 權 益 會 計        incorporated in these consolidated financial statements using
      法處理的聯營公司財務報表乃按本集團就                       the equity method of accounting. The financial statements of
      於類似情況下的交易及事件所採用者相同                       associates used for equity accounting purposes are prepared
      的 會 計 政 策 編 製。根 據 權 益 法，於 聯 營 公          using uniform accounting policies as those of the Group for
      司之投資初步按成本於綜合財務狀況表確                       like transactions and events in similar circumstances. Under
      認，並 於 其 後 就 確 認 本 集 團 應 佔 該 聯 營 公        the equity method, an investment in an associate is initially
      司 之 損 益 及 其 他 全 面 收 益 而 作 出 調 整。當        recognised in the consolidated statement of financial position
      本集團分佔聯營公司之虧損超出本集團於                       at cost and adjusted thereafter to recognise the Group’s share
      該聯營公司之權益時（包括實質上成為本集                      of the profit or loss and other comprehensive income of the
      團於該聯營公司投資淨額一部分之任何長                       associate. When the Group’s share of losses of an associate
      期權益），本集團終止確認其所佔進一步虧                      exceeds the Group’s interest in that associate (which includes
      損。僅 於 本 集 團 已 產 生 法 律 或 推 定 責 任，         any long-term interests that, in substance, form part of the
      或已代表該聯營公司支付款項之情況下，                       Group’s net investment in the associate), the Group discontinues
      方會確認進一步虧損。                               recognizing its share of further losses. Additional losses are
                                               recognised only to the extent that the Group has incurred legal
                                               or constructive obligations or made payments on behalf of the
                                               associate.

      於 被 投 資 方 成 為 一 間 聯 營 公 司 當 日，對 聯        An investment in an associate is accounted for using the equity
      營 公 司 之 投 資 採 用 權 益 法 入 賬。於 收 購 一        method from the date on which the investee becomes an
      間 聯 營 公 司 之 投 資 時，投 資 成 本 超 過 本 集        associate. On acquisition of the investment in an associate, any
      團分佔該被投資方可識別資產及負債公平                       excess of the cost of the investment over the Group’s share of
      淨 值 之 任 何 部 分 乃 確 認 為 商 譽，並 計 入 投        the net fair value of the identifiable assets and liabilities of the
      資 之 賬 面 值。本 集 團 所 佔 可 識 別 資 產 及 負        investee is recognised as goodwill, which is included within
      債於重新評估後之公平淨值與投資成本之                       the carrying amount of the investment. Any excess of the
      任 何 差 額，會 於 收 購 投 資 期 間 即 時 於 損 益        Group’s share of the net fair value of the identifiable assets and
      確認。                                      liabilities over the cost of the investment, after reassessment, is
                                               recognised immediately in profit or loss in the period in which

      存貨                                       Inventories

      存貨於各報告日期末按成本與可變現淨值                       Inventories are stated at the lower of cost and net realisable
      的較低者列賬。成本採用加權平均法計算。                      value at the end of each reporting date. Cost is calculated using
      可變現淨值指存貨的估計銷售價減所有估                       the weighted average method. Net realisable value represents
      計完成成本及進行銷售所必需的成本。                        the estimated selling price for inventories less all estimated costs
                                               of completion and costs necessary to make the sale.

      作出售用途之物業                                 Properties for sale

      作出售用途之竣工物業及作出售用途之在                       Completed properties for sale and properties under

      現 淨 值 中 較 低 者 列 賬。成 本 包 括 土 地 預 付        at the lower of cost and net realisable value. Cost comprises
      租 金 及 物 業 開 發 成 本。物 業 開 發 成 本 包 括        both the prepaid lease payments for land and development costs
      建 築 成 本、按 照 本 集 團 會 計 政 策 資 本 化 之        for the properties. Development costs of properties comprise
      借貸成本及開發期間產生之直接應佔成本。                      construction costs, borrowing costs capitalised according to
      進行銷售所必需的成本包括銷售直接應佔                       the Group’s accounting policy and directly attributable costs
      增量成本及本集團進行銷售時必然產生的                       incurred during the development period. Costs necessary to
      非 增 量 成 本。開 發 各 階 段 各 單 位 成 本 乃 採        make the sale include incremental costs directly attributable to
      用加權平均法釐定。                                sale and non-incremental costs which the Group must incur to

      在建物業於完成時轉撥至作出售用途之物業。                     Properties under development for sale are transferred to

      金融資產及金融負債初步按公平值計量，                       Financial assets and financial liabilities are initially measured
      惟按國際財務報告準則第 15 號初步計量之                    at fair value except for trade receivables which are initially
      貿 易 應 收 款 項 除 外。收 購 或 發 行 金 融 資 產        measured in accordance with IFRS 15. Transaction costs that are
      及金融負債（按公平值計入損益（「按公平值                     directly attributable to the acquisition or issue of financial assets
      計入損益」）之金融資產或金融負債除外）的                     and financial liabilities (other than financial assets or financial
      直接應佔交易成本加入或自初步確認之金                       liabilities at fair value through profit or loss (“FVTPL”)) are
      融資產或金融負債（倘合適）的公平值扣除。                     added to or deducted from the fair value of the financial assets

      負債直接應佔之交易成本乃即時於損益確認。                     Transaction costs directly attributable to the acquisition of
                                               financial assets or financial liabilities at FVTPL are recognised
                                               immediately in profit or loss.

      實際利息法是一種計算金融資產或金融負                       The effective interest method is a method of calculating the
      債的攤銷成本及於有關期間攤分利息收入                       amortised cost of a financial asset or financial liability and
      及 利 息 開 支 的 方 法。實 際 利 率 是 於 初 步 確        of allocating interest income and interest expense over the
      認時準確地透過金融資產或金融負債預計                       relevant period. The effective interest rate is the rate that exactly
      年期或（如適用）於較短期間內將估計日後                      discounts estimated future cash receipts and payments (including
      現金收款及付款（包括屬整體實際利率一部                      all fees and points paid or received that form an integral part of
      分 的 所 有 已 付 或 已 收 費 用 及 利 息、交 易 成        the effective interest rate, transaction costs and other premiums
      本及其他溢價或折讓）貼現為賬面淨值的利                      or discounts) through the expected life of the financial asset or
      率。                                       financial liability, or, where appropriate, a shorter period, to the
                                               net carrying amount on initial recognition.

      估的金融資產減值                                 under IFRS 9
      本集團根據國際財務報告準則第9號須予                       The Group performs impairment assessment under expected
      減 值 的 金 融 資 產（包 括 貿 易 及 其 他 應 收 款        credit loss (“ECL”) model on financial assets (including trade
      項、已抵押銀行存款以及銀行結餘及現金）                      and other receivables, pledged bank deposits, and bank
      按預期信貸虧損（「預期信貸虧損」）模式進                     balances and cash) which are subject to impairment under IFRS 9.
      行 減 值 評 估。預 期 信 貸 虧 損 的 金 額 於 各 報        The amount of ECL is updated at each reporting date to reflect
      告 日 期 更 新，以 反 映 信 貸 風 險 自 初 步 確 認        changes in credit risk since initial recognition.
      以來的變動。

      存續期預期信貸虧損指於相關工具之預計                       Lifetime ECL represents the ECL that will result from all possible
      年期內所有可能發生之違約事件所將產生                       default events over the expected life of the relevant instrument.
      之 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。反 之，12 個 月 預 期 信 貸         In contrast, 12-month ECL (“12m ECL”) represents the portion
      虧損（「12 個月預期信貸虧損」）指預期於報                   of lifetime ECL that is expected to result from default events
      告日期後 12 個月內可能發生之違約事件所                    that are possible within 12 months after the reporting date.
      將 產 生 之 部 分 存 續 期 預 期 信 貸 虧 損。評 估        Assessments are done based on the Group’s historical credit loss
      乃 按 本 集 團 過 往 信 貸 虧 損 經 驗 進 行，並 就        experience, adjusted for factors that are specific to the debtors,
      與 債 務 人 有 關 之 特 定 因 素、整 體 經 濟 情 況        general economic conditions and an assessment of both the
      以及對於報告日期之當前情況及預測之未                       current conditions at the reporting date as well as the forecast
      來情況兩者所作之評估作出調整。                          of future conditions.

      期信貸虧損。

      (v) 預期信貸虧損的計量及確認                     (v)   Measurement and recognition of ECL
          預期信貸虧損的計量為違約概率、違                       The measurement of ECL is a function of the probability
          約損失率（即違約損失程度）及違約風                      of default, loss given default (i.e. the magnitude of the
          險的函數。違約概率及違約損失率乃                       loss if there is a default) and the exposure at default.
          基於根據經前瞻性資料調整後的歷史                       The assessment of the probability of default and loss
          數據評估。預期信貸虧損的預估乃無                       given default is based on historical data adjusted by
          偏概率加權平均金額，以發生違約的                       forward-looking information. Estimation of ECL reflects
          風險為權重確定。                               an unbiased and probability-weighted amount that is
                                                 determined with the respective risks of default occurring
                                                 as the weights.

            一般而言，預期信貸虧損按本集團根                    Generally, the ECL is the difference between all
            據合約應收的所有合約現金流量與本                    contractual cash flows that are due to the Group in
            集團預計收取的現金流量的差額估計，                   accordance with the contract and the cash flows that
            並按初始確認時釐定的實際利率貼現。                   the Group expects to receive, discounted at the effective
                                                interest rate determined at initial recognition.

            倘預期信貸虧損按集體基準計量或迎                    Where ECL is measured on a collective basis or cater for
            合個別工具水平證據未必存在的情況，                   cases where evidence at the individual instrument level
            則金融工具按以下基準歸類：                       may not yet be available, the financial instruments are
                                                grouped on the following basis:

            —     逾期狀態；                         —     Past-due status;

            —     債務人的性質、規模及行業；及                —     Nature, size and industry of debtors; and

            —     外部信貸評級（倘有）。                   —     External credit ratings where available.

            歸類工作經管理層定期檢討，以確保                    The grouping is regularly reviewed by management to
            各組別成份繼續分擔類似信貸風險特                    ensure the constituents of each group continue to share
            性。                                  similar credit risk characteristics.

      備）。於各報告期末，本集團根據對所有相                      corresponding increase in equity (share options reserve). At the

      期 可 行 權 權 益 工 具 數 量 的 估 計。修 訂 原 估        of the number of equity instruments expected to vest based

      支 反 映 經 修 訂 的 估 計，並 相 應 調 整 購 股 權        The impact of the revision of the original estimates, if any, is

      於應用附註 3 所述的本集團會計政策時，本                    In the application of the Group’s accounting policies, which are
      公司董事須就並不容易從其他來源顯示的                       described in note 3, the directors of the Company are required
      資 產 及 負 債 的 賬 面 值 作 出 估 計 及 假 設。估        to make estimates and assumptions about the carrying amounts
      計及相關假設乃根據過往經驗及其他被認                       of assets and liabilities that are not readily apparent from other
      為 相 關 的 因 素 作 出。實 際 結 果 可 能 與 該 等        sources. The estimates and associated assumptions are based on
      估計不同。                                    historical experience and other factors that are considered to be
                                               relevant. Actual results may differ from these estimates.

      估 計 及 相 關 假 設 會 持 續 檢 討。倘 會 計 估 計        The estimates and underlying assumptions are reviewed on an
      的 修 訂 僅 對 估 計 修 訂 的 期 間 構 成 影 響，則        ongoing basis. Revisions to accounting estimates are recognised
      有 關 修 訂 於 該 期 間 確 認，或 倘 有 關 修 訂 對        in the period in which the estimate is revised if the revision
      本 期 間 及 未 來 期 間 均 構 成 影 響，則 修 訂 會        affects only that period, or in the period of the revision and
      於該修訂期間及未來期間確認。                           future periods if the revision affects both current and future
                                               periods.

      淄博曉碩企業管理有限公司                              Zibo Xiaoshuo enterprise management limited company

      於 2022 年 12 月 8 日，本 集 團 與 齊 魯 資 產 管       On 8 December 2022, the Group and Qilu Asset Management
      理（淄博）有限公司合資成立淄博曉碩企業                       (Zibo) Co., Ltd. jointly established Zibo Xiaoshuo Enterprise
      管 理有 限公 司（「淄 博 曉碩」），註 冊 資本 為              Management Co., LTD. (Zibo Xiaoshuo), with a registered
      人民幣 1 百萬元。本集團認購金額為人民幣                     capital of RMB1 million. The Group’s subscription amount is
      450,000 元，持 股 45%；於 2022 年 12 月 20        RMB450,000, holding 45%. On 20 December 2022, with an
      日，追 加 認 購 金 額 人 民 幣 60,000 元，持 有          additional subscription amount of RMB60,000, it holds 51% of
      淄博曉碩企業管理有限公司的 51% 股權。                     the equity of Zibo Xiaoshuo Enterprise Management Co., LTD.

      於 2023 年度，增資 352,846,000 元，仍持有            In 2023, with capital increase by RMB352,846,000, it still holds
      淄博曉碩企業管理有限公司的 51% 股權。                     51% of the equity of Zibo Xiaoshuo Enterprise Management
                                                Co., LTD.

      由 於 組 織 章 程 細 則 規 定，本 集 團 無 法 控 制         Due to the provisions of the articles of association, the
      董 事 會 及 股 東 層 級，故 一 直 作 為 一 間 聯 營         Group cannot control the level of the board of directors and
      公司入賬。                                     shareholders, so it is accounted for as an associate.

      於 2024 年 12 月 25 日，本 集 團 此 前 一 家 聯        On 25 December 2024 the shareholders of Zibo Xiaoshuo,
      營公司淄博曉碩的股東議決修訂組織章程                        one of the prior associate of the Group, resolved to amend the
      細則（組織章程細則）。根據組織章程細則                       article of association (AOA). Under the amendment of AOA, the
      的 修 訂 內 容，本 集 團 獲 得 對 淄 博 曉 碩 經 營         Group obtained the control of Zibo Xiaoshuo over the operating
      活 動 的 控 制 權，董 事 評 估 認 為 組 織 章 程 細         activities, the Directors assessed the amendments of AOA
      則的修訂導致對淄博曉碩的業務合併。                         resulted in the business combination of Zibo Xiaoshuo.

      委 聘 獨 立 專 業 估 值 師 進 行 有 關 估 值。該 金                    Group engages an independent professional valuer to perform
      額乃根據估計未來現金流量貼現至其現值                                   such valuation. The amount is determined based on the cash flow

      設，包 括 貼 現 率、最 終 增 長 率、預 算 收 益                        present value and requires the use of key assumptions, including
      及 毛 利 率，並 考 慮 到 相 關 行 業 增 長 預 測 及                    the discount rate, terminal growth rate, budgeted revenue and
      管理層就對市場發展的期望及市場方法，                                   gross margin taking into account the relevant industry growth

      本集團在中國的僱員乃中國各個地方政府                        The employees of the Group in the PRC are members of
      營 運 的 國 家 退 休 福 利 計 劃 的 成 員。本 集 團         stage-managed retirement benefit schemes operated by the
      須按工資成本的指定百分比向計劃供款，                        respective local governments in the PRC. The Group is required
      作 為 有 關 福 利 的 資 金。本 集 團 的 唯 一 責 任         to contribute a specified percentage of payroll costs to the
      是對該等計劃作出指定供款。                             schemes to fund the benefits. The only obligation of the

                                                contributions.
